Let's design algorithm more concretely:

For a given n and initial a:
1. Normalize to sum=1, nonneg.
2. Compute internal score exactly via FFT (maybe using evaluate_sequence occasionally).
3. Optimize:
   - Set τ = current M * 0.5 initially; maybe targetC = score/(2n). M = max c (since sum1). M ~0.8/n.
   - For iterations:
      - Compute c = conv(a,a) via FFT, clip small negatives.
      - M = max(c).
      - Update score; keep best (copy if improves).
      - Compute weight vector w for lags. We can use w_k = softmax((c_k - base)/τ) where base = quantile(high) or M. If all mass on max, use top q.
      - Gradient g = 2 * crosscorr(w,a) (vector length n). Then project gradient to tangent: g_proj = g - mean(g) (since simplex constraint). Or use g_proj = g - (g dot a?) /? For equality ∑a=1, subtract mean. Nonneg projection after step.
      - Step: v = a - η * g_proj. Clip to 0, normalize? Projection to simplex is better: v = a -η g_proj; a = proj_simplex(v). But if g_proj mean zero, sum v=1; projection due nonneg may change sum to1.
      - η: Use line search? Try a few candidate steps (e.g., 0.1,1,10 scaled by gradient norm). Or use adaptive: η = step_scale / (norm(g_proj)+eps). Choose based on desired movement. For max subgradient, can use η ~ 1e-3 * mean(a?)? Since a ~1/n, gradient ~? Let's estimate: For top lag k, g_i=2a_{k-i}. Mean g ~2 mean a^2? ~2/n^2? Wait a_i ~1/n, g_i ~1/n, mean ~1/n. g_proj magnitude ~1/n. To move a by O(1/n), step η~n? If a changes fraction maybe. If using g_proj directly, for n=2000, a entries ~0.0005, g~0.0005. Step η=1 changes O(0.0005), fine. But projection sort? Let's set base step η=1.0 and adapt based on score? Could just use η = temperature_param in [0.1,10]. We can try multiple.
   - Temperature anneal: τ = M * tau_ratio (e.g., 0.3 to 0.05). As optimize, focus tighter.
   - Randomization: every R iterations, perturb a with multiplicative noise, projected; keep if score not too bad or SA.
   - Periodically use "top-lag pair transfer" local moves.

Will this converge to <1.5? Need test concept. Since optimization of nonconvex with projected gradient may reduce peaks. Starting from harmonic or random maybe.

Maybe use "simulated annealing" around gradient: accept moves if score improves or with prob exp(-(Δ)/T_sa). But each move requires scoring. We can use local stochastic moves cheaper? Gradient moves already scoring each iter.

Gradient projected search likely better.

Let's derive internal score and gradient precisely and implement efficient.

Score internal:
def conv_max(a):
  n=len(a); N=1 << (2*n-2).bit_length(); fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N); cf=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N); creal=cf[:2*n-1]; due numerical negative small. m=creal.max()
  sum_a=a.sum(); if sum_a tiny return inf; return 2*n*m/(sum_a*sum_a)
We can normalize a to sum1 for internal; then score=2*n*max(c). If sum not exactly 1, include.
Gradient: given a (maybe not normalized), c=conv(a,a). We can compute p from c. Need gradient of logsumexp(c/τ) wrt a (without normalization). Then project for sum/ nonneg. We can also compute gradient of score directly: S=sum a. F=2n*C/S^2. Subgradient for C=max c_k: g= (2n/S^2)*( e_k 2a_shift - C? derivative S term: dF = (2n/S^2)(2freq? dc -2C dS/S). If w=onehot, dc=2a_shift. dS=sum δ= n? For vector gradient: g= (2n/S^2)(2 a_shift - (2C/S) 1). This should be tangent. For logsumexp, similar: L=logsumexp(c/τ); normalized? Projection to simplex efficient:
def proj_simplex(v):
  v=np.asarray(v,float)
  u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
  cssv=np.cumsum(u)-1
  ind=np.arange(1,len(u)+1)
  cond=u - cssv/ind >0
  if not cond.any(): return zeros? but sum? find max? Usually.
  rho=ind[cond][-1]; theta=cssv[cond][-1]/rho
  return np.maximum(v-theta,0)

```
def optimize_sequence(a0, budget_s, rng, tag, n_target=None, target_score=1.5):
   deadline = time.monotonic()+budget
   a = a0.copy(); a=clip...; if sum<1e-6 random
   # normalize sum=1 for internal; scale not matter
   a /= a.sum()
   best=a.copy(); best_score=internal_score(a)
   # maybe exact eval occasionally
   it=0; no_improve=0; last_print=...
   tau_rate=1.0; anneal to 0.05 over iterations or time
   beta_list = [...]
   while time < deadline:
      # compute c, grad using method
      mode_schedule = ...
      p = make_weights(c, M, tau_rate, mode, rng) # if not onehot
      g = gradient(p,a)
      g -= g.mean()
      scales = np.sqrt(np.mean(g*g))
      # line search over betas, maybe order random
      improved=False
      for beta in shuffled betas:
          v = a - beta * (g/scales)   # scaling so step ~beta
          if v.min() < -10: continue? projection handles
          cand=proj_simplex(v)
          sc=internal_score(cand) # but we can approximate? Need c for new.
          if finite and sc < best_score or sc < current_score:
              a=cand; current_score=sc; if sc<best_score: best=cand; no_improve=0; improved=True; break
      if not improved:
          # try random perturbation/SA
          if time%...
          current_score=internal_score(a)
      # anneal tau_rate, no_improve
```

```
def _next_pow2(m): ...
def _conv_max_from_fft(a):

   c=c[:2*n-1].real; c[c<0]=0; return c, float(c.max())

   lo = max(0, k-(n-1)); hi = min(n-1, k)
```

```
def _optimize(a, budget_s, rng, tag, target_score, print_prefix=''):
    deadline = time.monotonic()+budget_s
    a=np.asarray(a,float).copy(); a=np.clip(a,0,1000); sum; if sum<1e-6: a=np.ones_like(a);
    a/=a.sum()
    # add floor
    floor=1e-12
    c,M=_conv_score(a); current=2*n*M; best=a; best_score=current
    # SA temperature initial e.g., max(1e-4, best_score*0.02)
    tau_rate=1.0
    sa_temp = best_score*0.05
    no_improve_time=...
    it=0; last_repair=...
    while time<deadline:
       it+=1
       # every X iterations, print
       # recompute c if not have? We can compute at end. Initially compute.
       # choose mode
       if it % 4 ==0: mode='onehot'
       elif it % 4 ==1: mode='top'
       else: mode='softmax'
       tau_rate = max(0.05, 1.0 * (0.5** (it/200)))? Use time: tau_rate=0.05+0.95*exp(-it/2000)
       if mode=='onehot':
           # choose among lags with c >= M - 0.02*(M - small)
           threshold = M - 0.05*(M - 0.2*M) ? maybe.
           k=random weighted by max(0,c-threshold) or top.
           g=_spatial_grad_onehot(a,k)
       else:
           p=_make_weights(c, mode, rate=tau_rate, rng)
           g=_grad_fft(p,a)
       g -= g.mean()
       scale=np.sqrt(np.mean(g*g))+1e-12
       # line search order maybe random
       betas = _get_beta_list(it) # [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0]
       rng.shuffle(betas)
       improved=False
       for beta in betas:
           v = a - beta*(g/scale)
           cand = _proj_simplex(v)
           c_new, M_new = _conv_max(cand)
           sc_new = 2*n*M_new
           if finite:
              if sc_new < current or (sa accept) ...
       # update
       if not improved:
           # random perturbation
           if time last repair < 2s:
              cand=_perturb(a,rng,sa_temp?)
              ...
           else:
              # maybe skip to save
       # if no improvement for 10s, restart from best with larger random or different init
    return best,best_score
```

Perturbation methods:
```
def _perturb(a, rng, strength):
   n=a.size; r=a.copy()
   kind=rng.integers(3)
   if kind==0:
      # multiplicative lognormal
      r *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, strength, n))
   elif kind==1:
      # random direction
      d=rng.normal(0,1,n); d-=d.mean(); d/=np.sqrt(np.mean(d*d))
      r = a + strength * d * a.mean()
   else:
      # block
      w=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//50), max(2,n//5))); start=rng.integers(0,n-w); r[start:start+w]*=np.exp(rng.normal(0,strength))
   r=np.clip(r,0,None); s=r.sum(); if s>0: r/=s; else r=a; return r
```
Strength schedule: 0.05 to 0.5.

```
ncomp = rng.integers(2,8)
for j in range(ncomp):

   ph = rng.uniform(0,2π)
   amp = rng.uniform(0.1,1.0)/(j+1)

# normalize y to max abs 1
if max>0: y/=max
eps = rng.uniform(0.2,0.9)
a = 1 + eps*y
a = np.clip(a, 1e-6, None)
a/=a.sum()
```

Detailed code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

PINF = float('inf')

def _is_finite_list(seq): ...

def _pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length() # careful
```
Better:
```
def _next_power_of_2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`_fft_conv(a):`
```
n=a.size
N=_next_power_of_2(2*n-1)
fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)[:2*n-1].real
if c.size: c[(c<0)&(c>-1e-300)] = 0? just c=np.maximum(c,0)
return c
```
For large n, clip all negatives to 0; if significant negative due numerical? shouldn't.

Score:
```
def _score_from_fft(a):
  a=np.asarray(a,float)
  s=a.sum()
  if s<=1e-12: return inf
  # normalize? score scale invariant, but we can use normalized to avoid overflow
  an = a/s
  c=_fft_conv(an)
  return 2*an.size*float(c.max())
```
If a sum not1, after division. For speed, assume sum1, but use.

`_grad_fft(p,a):`
```
n=a.size; L=p.size
Nc=_next_power_of_2(3*n-2)
fa=np.fft.rfft(a,Nc)
fp=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], Nc)
t=np.fft.irfft(fa*fp, Nc)[:n+L-1].real
h=t[(n-1):(2*n-1)][::-1].copy()
return 2*h
```
Check if p symmetric not needed. If p includes negative? no.

`_grad_onehot(a,k):`
```
g=np.zeros(n)
lo=max(0,k-(n-1)); hi=min(n-1,k)
a_start=k-hi; a_end=k-lo # inclusive
g[lo:hi+1]=2*a[a_start:a_end+1][::-1]
return g
```

`_proj_simplex(v):`
```
def _proj_simplex(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,float).copy()
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    ind=np.arange(1, v.size+1, dtype=float)
    cond = (u - cssv/ind) > 0
    if not np.any(cond):
        # fallback: uniform plus? threshold above max
        rho = v.size
        theta = (np.sum(v))/rho? Actually if all cond false, use max? Standard if sum v <=0? Use uniform.
        return np.full(v.size, 1.0/v.size)
    rho = int(np.where(cond)[0][-1] + 1)
    theta = (cssv[rho-1]) / rho
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
```
If v sum exactly1, projection sum ~1; due numerical, we can normalize after to1: `r=r/ r.sum()` if sum>0. But projection theorem ensures; add.

`_normalize_nonneg(a):`
```
a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0); a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
s=a.sum()
if s<1e-12: return np.full_like(a,1.0/a.size)
a=a/s
return np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
```
Floor after normalization may change sum; renormalize: a=np.maximum(a,1e-15); a/sum.

SA accept probability:

```
def _accept_worse(sc_new, curr, temp):
   if sc_new <= curr: return True
   diff=sc_new-curr
   if temp <=1e-15: return False
   return rng.random() < math.exp(-diff/temp)
```

```
def _alpha_list(o, rng):
   vals = [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.6,3.2]
   # sample order: put one small one medium one large
   sel = [vals[1], vals[3], vals[5]]
   rng.shuffle(sel)
   return sel
```

Let's write `_make_weights` carefully:
```
def _make_weights(c, M, T, mode, rate, rng):
    L=c.size
    if mode == 'smooth':
        denom = max(M*rate, 1e-300)
        z = (c - M) / denom
        z = z - z.max()
        w = np.exp(z)
    elif mode == 'target':
        if T <= 0 or T >= M:
            thresh = M - 0.05*M
        else:
            # focus on violations but not all
            thresh = max(T, M - 0.1*max(M - T, 0.05*M))
        w = np.maximum(c - thresh, 0.0)
        w = w * w
    elif mode == 'top':
        med = float(np.median(c)) if L else 0
        span = M - med
        if span <= 1e-300:
            w = np.ones(L)
        else:
            top_frac = 0.02
            thresh = M - span*(1-top_frac)
            w = np.maximum(c - thresh, 0.0)
            w = w * w
    else:
        w = np.ones(L)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        w = np.ones(L)
        s = L
    w = w / s
    floor = 0.08
    w = floor * w + (1.0 - floor) / L
    s = w.sum()
    if s <=0 or not finite: w=np.ones(L)/L
    else: w/=s
    return w
```
If c has zeros and T high, w may only few lags. floor ensures global.

`_random_high_lag(c, rng)`:
```
M=c.max(); med=median; thresh=M - 0.8*(M-med) # top 20%
idx=np.where(c>=thresh)[0]
if idx.size==0: idx=[argmax]
w=np.maximum(c[idx]-thresh,0)+1e-30; w=w*w
return int(rng.choice(idx,p=w/w.sum()))
```
This can be O(n) each; fine. To avoid median each time, pass med? Not necessary.

Potential issue: `np.where` and choice with p can be slower but okay.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()
    total=max(120.0, float(budget_s)-10.0) # leave 10 for overhead/exact
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # try height_sequence_1 for global? If not None, maybe include initial.
    best=None; best_score=PINF
    config=[]
    if total >= 1500:
        config.append((4096, 0.30))
        config.append((10000, 0.70))
    elif total >= 600:
        config.append((6000, 1.0)) # Nc 16384? 3*6000=18000 ->32768? Actually 18000 next 32768; okay. Use 6000 if medium.
    else:
        config.append((2048,1.0))
    for n, frac in config:
       budget=total*frac
       if time.monotonic()-start < 20:
          best,best_score = _search_length(n,budget,rng,n, best,best_score)
    # final polish on best length if time>10
    if best is None:
       n=1024; best=_normalize_nonneg(np.ones(n)); best_score=...
    else:
       if time_left>30:
          n=best.size
          best,best_score = _optimize(best, time_left-20, rng, 'final', best_global=best, best_score_global=best_score)
    # exact eval if time_left >5
    out=[float(x) for x in best]
    try:
       if time.monotonic()+5 < start+2400? not know budget_s? use local deadline_start + budget_s
          exact=float(evaluate_sequence(out))
          if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < best_score: best_score=exact
          elif np.isfinite(exact): # if internal maybe, keep
             pass
    except Exception:
       pass
    if not np.isfinite(best_score): fallback uniform
    print final
    return out
```

Initial generator should include given global height_sequence resampled to n. But if global length large and n different, resampling may smooth but okay.
```
def _resample_sequence(seq, n):
    x=np.asarray(seq,float); x=np.clip(x,0,1000); finite; if x.size==0 return ones
    if x.size==n: return x
    if x.size==1: return np.full(n, float(x[0]))
    pos=np.linspace(0, x.size-1, n)
    old=np.arange(x.size)
    return np.interp(pos, old, x)
```
Values may be tiny; normalize later.

Generate initials code:
```
def _generate_initials(n, rng, count):
    out=[]
    def add(a):
       a=_normalize_nonneg(a)
       if a.size==n and np.isfinite(a).all(): out.append(a)
    # uniform
    add(np.ones(n))
    # given
    try:
       seq = height_sequence_1
       add(_resample_sequence(seq,n))
    except Exception: pass
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    for _ in range(count):
       kind=rng.integers(0,7)
       if kind==0: power
       elif kind==1: smooth random
       elif kind==2: exp
       elif kind==3: recip
       elif kind==4: block multiplicative
       elif kind==5: perm of power
       else: random full noise
       add(a)
    return out
```

We can avoid by `scored.sort()` but comparing arrays if scores equal could error. Use list of (score, index) sorted then map. Or implement stable manual. Let's carefully code `_optimize` without nested functions and variable complications. Since many lines, ensure variables updated.

Pseudo detailed:
```
def _optimize(a0, budget_s, rng, tag='', pool=None, best_global=None, best_score_global=PINF):
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+max(0,budget_s)
    a=_normalize_nonneg(np.asarray(a0,float))
    n=a.size
    c=_fft_conv(a); curr_M=float(c.max()); curr_score=2.0*n*curr_M if curr_M>0 else 0.0
    if best_global is None or len(best_global)!=n:
       best=a.copy(); best_score=curr_score
    else:
       best=np.asarray(best_global,float).copy(); best_score=float(best_score_global)
       if curr_score < best_score:
          best=a.copy(); best_score=curr_score
    it=0; last_best=t0; last_print=t0
    sa_base=max(0.02, best_score*0.05)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it +=1
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now - last_print >= 30:
          print(f'[{tag}] it={it} best={best_score:.6f} curr={curr_score:.6f}', flush=True)
          last_print=now
       o = min(1.0, (now-t0)/max(budget_s,1.0))
       sa_temp = max(1e-8, sa_base * math.exp(-4.0*o))
       target_M = best_score/(2.0*n) if best_score > 0 and np.isfinite(best_score) else curr_M*0.9
       if target_M <= 0: target_M = curr_M*0.9
       # mode
       if it % 5 == 0:
           mode='onehot'
       elif o < 0.25:
           mode='smooth'; rate=max(0.15, 0.8 - 2.0*o)
       elif o < 0.55:
           mode = 'smooth' if (it % 2 == 0) else 'target'
           rate=max(0.1, 0.5 - 2.0*o)
       elif o < 0.8:
           mode = 'target' if (it % 2 == 0) else 'top'
           rate=max(0.08, 0.3 - 2.0*o)
       else:
           mode = 'target' if (it % 3 == 0) else 'top'
           rate=max(0.05, 0.2 - 2.0*o)
       accepted=False
       # if no improve long -> restart before gradient maybe
       if now - last_best > 25.0:
          if pool is not None and len(pool)>0:
             src=pool[int(rng.integers(len(pool)))].copy()
          else:
             src=best.copy()
          a=_perturb(src, rng, 0.5)
          c=_fft_conv(a); curr_M=float(c.max()); curr_score=2.0*n*curr_M
          if curr_score < best_score:
             best=a.copy(); best_score=curr_score; last_best=now
          it=0; accepted=True
          continue
       # compute gradient
       if mode == 'onehot':
           k=_random_high_lag(c, rng)
           g=_grad_onehot(a,k)
       else:
           p=_make_weights(c, curr_M, target_M, mode, rate, rng)
           g=_grad_fft(p,a)
       g = np.asarray(g,float)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
           g=np.zeros(n)
       g -= g.mean()
       scale = math.sqrt(float(np.mean(g*g))) if n>0 else 0.0
       if scale > 1e-15:
          # sample alphas
          # base alphas depending on stage
          if o < 0.3: base_alphas = [0.1,0.3,1.0]
          elif o <0.7: base_alphas=[0.05,0.2,0.7]
          else: base_alphas=[0.02,0.1,0.4]
          idxs=list(range(len(base_alphas)))
          rng.shuffle(idxs)
          step = (g / scale) / float(n)
          for ai in idxs:
             alpha=float(base_alphas[ai])
             v=a - alpha*step
             cand=_fast_proj(v)
             if np.array_equal(cand,a):
                continue
             c_new=_fft_conv(cand); m_new=float(c_new.max()); sc_new=2.0*n*m_new
             if np.isfinite(sc_new):
                if sc_new < best_score:
                    best=cand.copy(); best_score=sc_new; last_best=time.monotonic()
                if sc_new <= curr_score or _sa_accept(sc_new, curr_score, sa_temp, rng):
                    a=cand; c=c_new; curr_M=m_new; curr_score=sc_new; accepted=True; break
       # local moves if not accepted
       if not accepted:
          # swaps 2
          for _ in range(2):
             k=_random_high_lag(c,rng)
             lo=max(0,k-(n-1)); hi=min(n-1,k)
             if hi-lo+1 <=1: continue
             ii=int(rng.integers(lo, hi+1)); jj=k-ii
             if jj<0 or jj>=n or ii==jj: continue
             cand=a.copy(); cand[ii],cand[jj]=cand[jj],cand[ii]
             c_new=_fft_conv(cand); sc_new=2.0*n*float(c_new.max())
             if np.isfinite(sc_new):
                if sc_new < best_score: best=cand.copy(); best_score=sc_new; last_best=time.monotonic()
                if sc_new <= curr_score or _sa_accept(sc_new,curr_score,sa_temp,rng):
                    a=cand; c=c_new; curr_score=sc_new; accepted=True; break
          # coordinate if not accepted
          if not accepted and n>0:
             # choose index with large value or random
             if rng.random()<0.5 and n>5:
                 # top quartile
                 thresh=np.percentile(a,75)
                 candidates=np.where(a>=thresh)[0]
                 if candidates.size>0:
                     ii=int(candidates[int(rng.integers(candidates.size))])
                 else: ii=int(np.argmax(a))
             else:
                 ii=int(rng.integers(n))
             aii=a[ii]
             # choose factor: if ai average, try high/low
             if rng.random()<0.5:
                 factor = 0.5 if ai > a.mean() else 2.0
             else:
                 factor = float(rng.uniform(0.4, 2.5))
             cand=a.copy(); cand[ii]*=factor; cand=_normalize_nonneg(cand)
             c_new=_fft_conv(cand); sc_new=2.0*n*float(c_new.max())
             if np.isfinite(sc_new):
                if sc_new < best_score: best=cand.copy(); best_score=sc_new; last_best=time.monotonic()
                if sc_new <= curr_score or _sa_accept(sc_new,curr_score,sa_temp,rng):
                    a=cand; c=c_new; curr_score=sc_new; accepted=True
       if not accepted:
          # perturbation
          sigma=0.15 + 0.25*min(1.0, o)
          a=_perturb(a, rng, sigma)
          c=_fft_conv(a); curr_M=float(c.max()); curr_score=2.0*n*curr_M
          if curr_score < best_score:
             best=a.copy(); best_score=curr_score; last_best=time.monotonic()
          # do not "accept" meaning continue; loop will use new current
    # final clamp
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    if 2.0*a.size*float(_fft_conv(a).max()) < best_score:
       best=a.copy(); best_score=...
    return _normalize_nonneg(best), float(best_score)
```

```
def _fast_proj(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
       v=np.where(np.isfinite(v),v,0.0)
    vmin=float(v.min())
    if vmin >= 0:
       s=float(v.sum())
       if s > 1e-15:
          r=v/s
       else:
          r=np.full(v.size,1.0/v.size)
    else:
       r=_proj_simplex(v)
    r=np.maximum(r,1e-15)
    s=float(r.sum())
    if s<=0 or not finite: r=np.full(r.size,1.0/r.size)
    else: r=r/s
    return r
```

`_normalize_nonneg`:
```
def _normalize_nonneg(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).copy()
   if a.ndim !=1 or a.size==0: return np.ones(16)
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   s=float(a.sum())
   if s <= 1e-15:
      a=np.full(a.size,1.0/max(1,a.size))
   else:
      a=a/s
   a=np.maximum(a,1e-15)
   s=float(a.sum())
   a=a/s
   return a
```
All outputs positive. Good.

`_sa_accept` top-level:
```
def _sa_accept(new, curr, temp, rng):
   if new <= curr: return True
   if temp <= 1e-15: return False
   # avoid overflow
   diff=new-curr
   if diff > 50*temp: return False
   return bool(rng.random() < math.exp(-diff/temp))
```

`_perturb` uses rng. Ensure output normalized.
```
def _perturb(a, rng, sigma):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size
   sigma=float(max(1e-6, sigma))
   kind=int(rng.integers(3))
   if kind==0:
      r=a*np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma,n))
   elif kind==1:
      d=rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n); d-=d.mean()
      dn=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d*d)))
      if dn >0: d/=dn
      r=a + (sigma/float(n))*d
   else:
      r=a.copy()
      maxw=max(2,n//3); minw=max(1,n//100)
      w=int(rng.integers(minw, maxw+1))
      st=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
      r[st:st+w]*=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma,w))
   return _normalize_nonneg(r)
```

```
def _generate_initials(n, rng, count):

       hseq=height_sequence_1
       if hseq is not None:

    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    for idx in range(count):
       kind=int(rng.integers(0,8))
       a=np.ones(n)
       if kind==0:
          alpha=float(rng.uniform(0.1,8.0)); q=float(rng.uniform(-1.2,1.2))
          a=(1.0+alpha*t)**q
       elif kind==1:
          alpha=float(rng.uniform(0.1,8.0)); q=float(rng.uniform(-1.2,1.2))
          a=(1.0+alpha*(1.0-t))**q
       elif kind==2:

       elif kind==3:
          c=float(rng.uniform(0.3,0.7)); k=float(rng.uniform(-3,3)); a=1.0/(1.0+np.exp(-k*(t-c)))
       elif kind==4:

          y=np.zeros(n)
          nc=int(rng.integers(2,7))
          for j in range(nc):
             f=float(rng.choice([1,2,3,4,6,8,12,16])) # choice from list, rng.choice okay
             ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)); amp=float(rng.uniform(0.1,1.0)/(j+1))
             y += amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*t + ph)
          mx=float(np.max(np.abs(y))) if y.size else 1.0
          if mx>0: y/=mx
```

`_resample_sequence`:
```
def _resample_sequence(seq, n):
    x=np.asarray(seq,float)
    if x.ndim !=1 or x.size==0:
       return np.ones(n)
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x),x,0.0); x=np.clip(x,0,1000)
    if x.size==1:
       v=float(x[0]); return np.full(n, v if v>0 else 1.0)
    pos=np.linspace(0,x.size-1,n)
    return np.interp(pos, np.arange(x.size), x)
```

Define `_next_power_of_two`:
```
def _next_power_of_two(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```
For m=3: (2).bit_length=2 ->4 good.

`_fft_conv`:
```
def _fft_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([],float)
    L=2*n-1
    N=_next_power_of_two(L)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L].real
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```
If a has negative? we ensure positive.

```
    n=a.size
    if n<=0: return _INF
    c=_fft_conv(a)
    if c.size==0: return _INF
    m=float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m): return _INF
    return 2.0*float(n)*m
```

`_make_weights`: If mode is unknown, smooth? Use default.
```
def _make_weights(c, M, target_M, mode, rate, rng):
    L = int(c.size)
    w = np.ones(L, dtype=float)
    if mode == 'smooth':
       denom = max(float(M)*float(rate), 1e-300)
       z = (c - M)/denom
       z = z - float(np.max(z))
       w = np.exp(z)
    elif mode == 'target':
       if M <= 0: w=np.ones(L)
       else:
          if target_M >= M or not np.isfinite(target_M):
              thresh = M - 0.05*M
          else:
              thresh = max(target_M, M - 0.1*(M - target_M))
          w = np.maximum(c - thresh, 0.0)
          w = w*w
    elif mode == 'top':
       med = float(np.median(c)) if L else 0.0
       span = M - med
       if span <= 1e-300:
          w = np.ones(L)
       else:
          top_frac = 0.02
          thresh = M - span*(1.0 - top_frac)
          w = np.maximum(c - thresh, 0.0)
          w = w*w
    else: # onehot? not here
       w = np.ones(L)
    s=float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0: w=np.ones(L); s=L
    w = w/s
    floor = 0.08
    w = floor*w + (1.0-floor)/L
    s=float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0: return np.ones(L)/L
    return w/s
```
Floor maybe 0.1. Good.

`_random_high_lag`:
```
def _random_high_lag(c, rng):
    L=c.size
    if L==0: return 0
    M=float(c.max())
    if M <=0: return int(L//2)
    med=float(np.median(c))
    if med >= M: med=0.0
    thresh=M - 0.8*(M - med)
    idx=np.where(c >= thresh)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
       return int(np.argmax(c))
    w=np.maximum(c[idx]-thresh,0.0)
    w=w*w + 1e-300
    return int(rng.choice(idx, p=w/w.sum()))
```
`rng.choice` with p array length >0. Good.

`_proj_simplex`: Need if n=0. Use:
```
def _proj_simplex(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    n=v.size
    if n==0: return v
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    ind=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    cond = u - cssv/ind > 0
    if not np.any(cond):
       return np.full(n, 1.0/n)
    rho = int(np.where(cond)[0][-1] + 1)
    theta = cssv[rho-1]/rho
    r=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    s=float(r.sum())
    if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    return r/s
```

`_fast_proj` uses `_proj_simplex`, floor.


`_grad_fft`:
```
def _grad_fft(p,a):
   n=a.size; L=p.size
   if n==0: return np.array([])
   N=_next_power_of_two(n+L-1)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   fp=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],N)
   t=np.fft.irfft(fa*fp,N)[:n+L-1].real
   h=t[(n-1):(2*n-1)][::-1].copy()
   return 2.0*h
```
For n=1,L=1: slice (0):(1), good.

`_sa_accept`:
```
def _sa_accept(new, curr, temp, rng):
   if new <= curr: return True
   if temp <= 0 or not np.isfinite(temp): return False
   diff = new - curr
   if not np.isfinite(diff): return False
   if diff > 40.0*temp: return False
   return bool(rng.random() < math.exp(-diff/temp))
```

```
def _search_length(n, budget_s, rng, tag, best_global, best_score_global, hard_end=None):
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+budget_s
    if deadline > hard_end: deadline=hard_end
    count = 8 if budget_s < 200 else 24
    pool = _generate_initials(n, rng, count)
    scored=[]
    for i in range(len(pool)):
       sc=_internal_score(pool[i])
       if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc,i))
    scored.sort()
    if not scored:
       a=_normalize_nonneg(np.ones(n)); scored=[(2.0,n?)]? internal score returns 2 for uniform. scored=[(_internal_score(a),0)] pool[0]=a
    top_k = 2 if budget_s > 400 else 1
    top=[pool[i] for sc,i in scored[:min(top_k,len(scored))]]
    local_best=top[0].copy(); local_score=scored[0][0]
    # maybe include global same length if better? Can't if different.
    run_budget = max(30.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) / max(1, len(top)))
    for idx in range(len(top)):
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       # leave at least 20s for final? 
       rb = run_budget
       # if it's last run, rb = min(rb, deadline - now - 20) ? but need final set.
       local_best, local_score = _optimize(top[idx], rb, rng, tag=f'{tag}_{idx}', pool=top, best_global=local_best, best_score_global=local_score)
    # final polish if time left
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20 and local_best.size == n:
       rb = deadline - time.monotonic() - 20
       if rb > 30:
          local_best, local_score = _optimize(local_best, rb, rng, tag=tag+'_polish', pool=top, best_global=local_best, best_score_global=local_score)
    if local_score < best_score_global:
       best_global = local_best.copy(); best_score_global = local_score
    return best_global, best_score_global
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    total_budget=float(budget_s)
    search_budget=max(60.0, total_budget - 25.0)
    hard_end=t_start + total_budget - 10.0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best=None; best_score=_INF
    if search_budget >= 1500:
       plans=[(4096,0.30),(10000,0.70)]
    elif search_budget >= 700:
       plans=[(7000,1.0)]
    elif search_budget >= 300:
       plans=[(4000,1.0)]
    else:
       plans=[(1024,1.0)]
    for n,frac in plans:
       b=budget? review time
       b=search_budget*frac
       if time.monotonic() >= hard_end - 20: break
       if b < 20: continue
       best,best_score = _search_length(n,min(b,hard_end-time.monotonic()),rng,str(n),best,best_score,hard_end)
    if best is None:
       best=_normalize_nonneg(np.ones(1024)); best_score=_internal_score(best)
    # final polish global best with length n_best
    if time.monotonic() < hard_end - 10:
       rb=hard_end - time.monotonic() - 12
       if rb > 30:
          best,best_score = _optimize(best, rb, rng, 'final', best_global=best, best_score_global=best_score)
    out=[float(x) for x in best]
    if time.monotonic() + 5.0 < hard_end:

    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
       best=np.ones(64,dtype=float); out=[float(x) for x in best]; best_score=2.0
    print(f"[final] length={len(out)} internal={best_score:.6f}", flush=True)
    return out
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function heights minimizing

    score(a) = 2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2

using Fourier-accelerated smooth projected gradient descent, top-lag
constraint weighting, random coordinated local moves, and multiple
sequence lengths / restarts.  Internal evaluations are FFT-based; the
external evaluator is used only occasionally as a safe final check.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

_INF = float("inf")


def _next_power_of_two(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(16, dtype=float)
    a = a.copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        a = np.full(a.size, 1.0 / max(1, a.size))
    else:
        a = a / s
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a = np.full(a.size, 1.0 / max(1, a.size))
    else:
        a = a / s
    return a


def _fft_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_power_of_two(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:L].real
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def _internal_score(a):
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return _INF
    c = _fft_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return _INF
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return _INF
    return 2.0 * float(n) * m


def _resample_sequence(seq, n):
    x = np.asarray(seq, dtype=float)
    if x.ndim != 1 or x.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if x.size == 1:
        v = float(x[0])
        if v <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(v):
            v = 1.0
        return np.full(n, v, dtype=float)
    pos = np.linspace(0.0, x.size - 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(pos, np.arange(x.size, dtype=float), x)


def _proj_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    ind = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=float)
    cond = u - cssv / ind > 0.0
    if not np.any(cond):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=float)
    rho = int(np.where(cond)[0][-1] + 1)
    theta = cssv[rho - 1] / float(rho)
    r = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(r.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=float)
    return r / s


def _fast_project(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    vmin = float(v.min())
    if vmin >= 0.0:
        s = float(v.sum())
        if s > 1e-15:
            r = v / s
        else:
            r = np.full(v.size, 1.0 / max(1, v.size))
    else:
        r = _proj_simplex(v)
    r = np.maximum(r, 1e-15)
    s = float(r.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        r = np.full(r.size, 1.0 / max(1, r.size))
    else:
        r = r / s
    return r


def _grad_onehot(a, k):
    n = a.size
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n == 0:
        return g
    k = int(k)
    k = min(max(k, 0), 2 * n - 2)
    lo = max(0, k - (n - 1))
    hi = min(n - 1, k)
    if hi < lo:
        return g
    a_start = k - hi
    a_end = k - lo
    g[lo:hi + 1] = 2.0 * a[a_start:a_end + 1][::-1]
    return g


def _grad_fft(p, a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    n = a.size
    L = p.size
    if n == 0 or L == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    N = _next_power_of_two(n + L - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
    t = np.fft.irfft(fa * fp, N)[:n + L - 1].real
    h = t[(n - 1):(2 * n - 1)][::-1].copy()
    return 2.0 * h


def _make_weights(c, M, target_M, mode, rate, rng):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    L = int(c.size)
    w = np.ones(L, dtype=float)

    if mode == "smooth":
        denom = max(float(M) * float(rate), 1e-300)
        z = (c - M) / denom
        z = z - float(np.max(z))
        w = np.exp(z)
    elif mode == "target":
        if M <= 0.0:
            w = np.ones(L, dtype=float)
        else:
            if not np.isfinite(target_M) or target_M >= M:
                thresh = M - 0.05 * M
            else:
                thresh = max(target_M, M - 0.1 * (M - target_M))
            w = np.maximum(c - thresh, 0.0)
            w = w * w
    elif mode == "top":
        med = float(np.median(c)) if L else 0.0
        span = M - med
        if span <= 1e-300:
            w = np.ones(L, dtype=float)
        else:
            top_frac = 0.02
            thresh = M - span * (1.0 - top_frac)
            w = np.maximum(c - thresh, 0.0)
            w = w * w
    else:
        w = np.ones(L, dtype=float)

    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones(L, dtype=float)
        s = float(L)
    w = w / s

    floor = 0.08
    w = floor * w + (1.0 - floor) / float(L)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=float) / float(L)
    return w / s


def _random_high_lag(c, rng):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    L = int(c.size)
    if L == 0:
        return 0
    M = float(c.max())
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return L // 2
    med = float(np.median(c))
    if med >= M:
        med = 0.0
    thresh = M - 0.8 * (M - med)
    idx = np.where(c >= thresh)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        return int(np.argmax(c))
    w = np.maximum(c[idx] - thresh, 0.0)
    w = w * w + 1e-300
    w = w / float(w.sum())
    return int(rng.choice(idx, p=w))


def _sa_accept(new_score, curr_score, temp, rng):
    if new_score <= curr_score:
        return True
    if temp <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(temp):
        return False
    diff = new_score - curr_score
    if not np.isfinite(diff):
        return False
    if diff > 40.0 * temp:
        return False
    return bool(rng.random() < math.exp(-diff / temp))


def _perturb(a, rng, sigma):
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    sigma = float(max(1e-6, sigma))
    kind = int(rng.integers(3))

    if kind == 0:
        r = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
    elif kind == 1:
        d = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
        d -= float(d.mean())
        dn = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d * d)))
        if dn > 0.0:
            d = d / dn
        r = a + (sigma / float(n)) * d
    else:
        r = a.copy()
        lo_w = max(1, n // 100)
        hi_w = max(lo_w + 1, n // 3)
        w = int(rng.integers(lo_w, hi_w + 1))
        st = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
        r[st:st + w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, w))

    return _normalize_nonneg(r)


def _generate_initials(n, rng, count):
    pool = []
    pool.append(_normalize_nonneg(np.ones(n, dtype=float)))

    try:
        hseq = height_sequence_1
        if hseq is not None:
            pool.append(_normalize_nonneg(_resample_sequence(hseq, n)))
    except Exception:
        pass

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    for _ in range(count):
        kind = int(rng.integers(8))
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=float)

        if kind == 0:
            alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 8.0))
            q = float(rng.uniform(-1.2, 1.2))
            a = (1.0 + alpha * t) ** q

        elif kind == 1:
            alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 8.0))
            q = float(rng.uniform(-1.2, 1.2))
            a = (1.0 + alpha * (1.0 - t)) ** q

        elif kind == 2:
            lam = float(rng.uniform(-1.5, 1.5))
            a = np.exp(lam * t)

        elif kind == 3:
            cc = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 0.7))
            kk = float(rng.uniform(-3.0, 3.0))
            a = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-kk * (t - cc)))

        elif kind == 4:
            y = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
            nc = int(rng.integers(2, 7))
            for j in range(nc):
                f = float(rng.choice([1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8, 12, 16]))
                ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
                amp = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 1.0)) / float(j + 1)
                y += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * t + ph)
            mx = float(np.max(np.abs(y))) if y.size else 1.0
            if mx > 0.0:
                y = y / mx
            eps = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.85))
            a = 1.0 + eps * y

        elif kind == 5:
            a = rng.normal(1.0, 0.3, n)

        elif kind == 6:
            nb = int(rng.integers(3, 12))
            for _ in range(nb):
                w = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 50), max(2, n // 5) + 1))
                st = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
                a[st:st + w] *= float(rng.lognormal(0.0, 0.4))

        else:
            alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 5.0))
            q = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0))
            a = (1.0 + alpha * t) ** q
            a = rng.permutation(a)

        a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
        if a.size == n:
            pool.append(a)

    return pool


def _optimize(a0, budget_s, rng, tag="", pool=None, best_global=None, best_score_global=_INF):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    a = _normalize_nonneg(np.asarray(a0, dtype=float))
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        a = _normalize_nonneg(np.ones(16, dtype=float))
        n = a.size

    c = _fft_conv(a)
    curr_M = float(c.max())
    curr_score = 2.0 * float(n) * curr_M

    if best_global is None or getattr(best_global, "size", 0) != n:
        best = a.copy()
        best_score = curr_score
    else:
        best = _normalize_nonneg(np.asarray(best_global, dtype=float))
        best_score = float(best_score_global)
        if curr_score < best_score:
            best = a.copy()
            best_score = curr_score

    it = 0
    last_best = t0
    last_print = t0
    sa_base = max(0.02, 0.05 * best_score if np.isfinite(best_score) else 0.1)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        now = time.monotonic()
        elapsed = now - t0
        if elapsed - last_print >= 30.0:
            print(f"[{tag}] it={it} best={best_score:.6f} curr={curr_score:.6f}", flush=True)
            last_print = now

        o = min(1.0, elapsed / max(1.0, float(budget_s)))
        sa_temp = max(1e-8, sa_base * math.exp(-4.0 * o))

        target_M = best_score / (2.0 * float(n)) if np.isfinite(best_score) and best_score > 0.0 else curr_M * 0.9
        if target_M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(target_M):
            target_M = curr_M * 0.9

        mode = "smooth"
        rate = 0.4
        if it % 5 == 0:
            mode = "onehot"
            rate = 0.2
        elif o < 0.25:
            mode = "smooth"
            rate = max(0.15, 0.8 - 2.0 * o)
        elif o < 0.55:
            mode = "smooth" if (it % 2 == 0) else "target"
            rate = max(0.10, 0.5 - 2.0 * o)
        elif o < 0.8:
            mode = "target" if (it % 2 == 0) else "top"
            rate = max(0.08, 0.3 - 2.0 * o)
        else:
            mode = "target" if (it % 3 == 0) else "top"
            rate = max(0.05, 0.2 - 2.0 * o)

        if now - last_best > 25.0:
            if pool is not None and len(pool) > 0:
                src = pool[int(rng.integers(len(pool)))].copy()
            else:
                src = best.copy()
            a = _perturb(src, rng, 0.5)
            c = _fft_conv(a)
            curr_M = float(c.max())
            curr_score = 2.0 * float(n) * curr_M
            if curr_score < best_score:
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = curr_score
                last_best = time.monotonic()
            it = 0
            continue

        accepted = False

        if mode == "onehot":
            k = _random_high_lag(c, rng)
            g = _grad_onehot(a, k)
        else:
            p = _make_weights(c, curr_M, target_M, mode, rate, rng)
            g = _grad_fft(p, a)

        g = np.asarray(g, dtype=float)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        g -= float(g.mean())
        scale = math.sqrt(float(np.mean(g * g))) if n > 0 else 0.0

        if scale > 1e-15:
            if o < 0.3:
                alphas = [0.1, 0.3, 1.0]
            elif o < 0.7:
                alphas = [0.05, 0.2, 0.7]
            else:
                alphas = [0.02, 0.1, 0.4]

            idxs = list(range(len(alphas)))
            rng.shuffle(idxs)
            unit_step = (g / scale) / float(n)

            for ai in idxs:
                alpha = float(alphas[ai])
                v = a - alpha * unit_step
                cand = _fast_project(v)
                c_new = _fft_conv(cand)
                m_new = float(c_new.max())
                sc_new = 2.0 * float(n) * m_new
                if not np.isfinite(sc_new):
                    continue
                if sc_new < best_score:
                    best = cand.copy()
                    best_score = sc_new
                    last_best = time.monotonic()
                if sc_new <= curr_score or _sa_accept(sc_new, curr_score, sa_temp, rng):
                    a = cand
                    c = c_new
                    curr_M = m_new
                    curr_score = sc_new
                    accepted = True
                    break

        if not accepted:
            if it % 3 == 0:
                k = _random_high_lag(c, rng)
                lo = max(0, k - (n - 1))
                hi = min(n - 1, k)
                if hi - lo + 1 > 1:
                    ii = int(rng.integers(lo, hi + 1))
                    jj = k - ii
                    if 0 <= jj < n and ii != jj:
                        cand = a.copy()
                        cand[ii], cand[jj] = cand[jj], cand[ii]
                        c_new = _fft_conv(cand)
                        m_new = float(c_new.max())
                        sc_new = 2.0 * float(n) * m_new
                        if np.isfinite(sc_new):
                            if sc_new < best_score:
                                best = cand.copy()
                                best_score = sc_new
                                last_best = time.monotonic()
                            if sc_new <= curr_score or _sa_accept(sc_new, curr_score, sa_temp, rng):
                                a = cand
                                c = c_new
                                curr_M = m_new
                                curr_score = sc_new
                                accepted = True

            elif it % 3 == 1 and n > 1:
                if rng.random() < 0.6:
                    th = 1.5 * float(a.mean())
                    cand_idx = np.where(a >= th)[0]
                    if cand_idx.size > 0:
                        ii = int(cand_idx[int(rng.integers(cand_idx.size))])
                    else:
                        ii = int(np.argmax(a))
                else:
                    ii = int(rng.integers(n))

                factor = float(rng.uniform(0.4, 2.5))
                cand = a.copy()
                cand[ii] *= factor
                cand = _normalize_nonneg(cand)
                c_new = _fft_conv(cand)
                m_new = float(c_new.max())
                sc_new = 2.0 * float(n) * m_new
                if np.isfinite(sc_new):
                    if sc_new < best_score:
                        best = cand.copy()
                        best_score = sc_new
                        last_best = time.monotonic()
                    if sc_new <= curr_score or _sa_accept(sc_new, curr_score, sa_temp, rng):
                        a = cand
                        c = c_new
                        curr_M = m_new
                        curr_score = sc_new
                        accepted = True

        if not accepted:
            sigma = 0.15 + 0.35 * min(1.0, o)
            a = _perturb(a, rng, sigma)
            c = _fft_conv(a)
            curr_M = float(c.max())
            curr_score = 2.0 * float(n) * curr_M
            if curr_score < best_score:
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = curr_score
                last_best = time.monotonic()

    best = _normalize_nonneg(best)
    exact_candidate_score = _internal_score(best)
    if np.isfinite(exact_candidate_score) and exact_candidate_score < best_score:
        best_score = exact_candidate_score
    return best, float(best_score)


def _search_length(n, budget_s, rng, tag, best_global, best_score_global, hard_end):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    if deadline > hard_end:
        deadline = hard_end

    count = 10 if budget_s < 250 else 24
    pool = _generate_initials(n, rng, count)
    if not pool:
        pool = [_normalize_nonneg(np.ones(n, dtype=float))]

    scored = []
    for i in range(len(pool)):
        sc = _internal_score(pool[i])
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, i))

    scored.sort()
    if not scored:
        good = _normalize_nonneg(np.ones(n, dtype=float))
        pool = [good]
        scored = [(_internal_score(good), 0)]

    top_k = 2 if budget_s > 400 else 1
    if top_k > len(scored):
        top_k = len(scored)
    top = [pool[i] for _, i in scored[:top_k]]

    local_best = top[0].copy()
    local_score = float(scored[0][0])

    run_budget = max(30.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) / float(max(1, len(top))))
    for i in range(len(top)):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        rb = run_budget
        # leave a little room for the final polish
        if i == len(top) - 1:
            rb = min(rb, deadline - time.monotonic() - 20.0)
            if rb < 30.0:
                continue
        local_best, local_score = _optimize(
            top[i],
            rb,
            rng,
            tag=f"{tag}_{i}",
            pool=pool,
            best_global=local_best,
            best_score_global=local_score,
        )

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0 and local_best.size == n:
        rb = deadline - time.monotonic() - 25.0
        if rb > 30.0:
            local_best, local_score = _optimize(
                local_best,
                rb,
                rng,
                tag=f"{tag}_polish",
                pool=pool,
                best_global=local_best,
                best_score_global=local_score,
            )

    if local_score < best_score_global:
        best_global = local_best.copy()
        best_score_global = local_score

    return best_global, best_score_global


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    search_budget = max(60.0, total_budget - 25.0)
    hard_end = t_start + total_budget - 10.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best = None
    best_score = _INF

    if search_budget >= 1500.0:
        plans = [(4096, 0.30), (10000, 0.70)]
    elif search_budget >= 700.0:
        plans = [(7000, 1.0)]
    elif search_budget >= 300.0:
        plans = [(4000, 1.0)]
    else:
        plans = [(1024, 1.0)]

    for n, frac in plans:
        if time.monotonic() >= hard_end - 20.0:
            break
        b = search_budget * frac
        b = min(b, hard_end - time.monotonic())
        if b < 20.0:
            continue
        best, best_score = _search_length(n, b, rng, f"len{n}", best, best_score, hard_end)

    if best is None:
        best = _normalize_nonneg(np.ones(1024, dtype=float))
        best_score = _internal_score(best)

    # Final polish for whatever length currently is best.
    if time.monotonic() < hard_end - 20.0 and best.size > 0:
        rb = hard_end - time.monotonic() - 20.0
        if rb > 30.0:
            best, best_score = _optimize(
                best,
                rb,
                rng,
                tag="final_polish",
                pool=None,
                best_global=best,
                best_score_global=best_score,
            )

    out = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(best, dtype=float)]

    # Optional external authoritative check if there is comfortable margin.
    if time.monotonic() + 60.0 < t_start + total_budget:
        try:
            exact_score = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
            if np.isfinite(exact_score):
                if exact_score < best_score:
                    best_score = exact_score
        except Exception:
            pass

    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best = np.ones(64, dtype=float)
        out = [float(x) for x in best]
        best_score = 2.0

    print(f"[final] length={len(out)} best_score={best_score:.6f}", flush=True)
    return out
```
